⭐️\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eIn case you are looking for a list of all elements you need to have/to test before launching your site/HTML page to production, take a look on the → \\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/thedaviddias/Front-End-Checklist\\\"\\u003eFront-End Checklist\\u003c/a\\u003e.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eHow to use the Design Checklist?\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-how-to-use-the-design-checklist\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: How to use the Design Checklist?\\\" href=\\\"#how-to-use-the-design-checklist\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eWhen comes the moment where developers discover new web designs, before converting them to code, some important elements may be missing. The Front-End Design Checklist is a tool for Front-End developers and Web Designers which aim to help both to work in a seamlessly way.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eYou can share that checklist to Web Designers to ensure time will be saved at the delivery time or you can use it to review all elements delivered by the creative team and ensure everything is correct before digging into the code integration.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eWhy you need to use the Design Checklist?\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-why-you-need-to-use-the-design-checklist\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: Why you need to use the Design Checklist?\\\" href=\\\"#why-you-need-to-use-the-design-checklist\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eEnsure all points are taken into consideration by the Creative Team\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eHaving a document where Web Designers and Developers can rely to ensure a better communication and coherence in the way they interact.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eBecause it's easy to forget some important elements when you are pushed by short timelines\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eAvoid discovering issues after the creative team is already working on another project.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eTo show the complementary work between a Web Designer and a Front-End Developer\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003chr\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e1. - Design requirements\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-1---design-requirements\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" following some rules and taking into consideration that the project is not only a graphic project but a web project too. The next sections are crucial for any web project.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e1.1 - Grid system\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-11---grid-system\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 1.1 - Grid system\\\" href=\\\"#11---grid-system\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca target=\\\"_blank\\\" rel=\\\"noopener noreferrer\\\" href=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/grid-system.png\\\"\\u003e\\u003cimg src=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/grid-system.png\\\" alt=\\\"Grid system\\\" style=\\\"max-width: 100%;\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul class=\\\"contains-task-list\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e A \\u003cstrong\\u003egrid\\u003c/strong\\u003e is explicitly provided in the design, and the details of the grid are present in the technical specification (width, gutters, number of columns…). The Web Designer can keep the grid in a transparent layer and use it on all his project.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cblockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eℹ️ \\u003ca href=\\\"https://guideguide.me/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eGuide Guide\\u003c/a\\u003e is a plugin for Photoshop that can help you easily build your grid.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/blockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cblockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eℹ️ On Sketch, you can use the integrated “\\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.sketchapp.com/docs/canvas/rulers-guides-grids/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eMake Grid Tool\\u003c/a\\u003e” to design your desired grid.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/blockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e \\u003cstrong\\u003eBe familiar with the grid system\\u003c/strong\\u003e you’ll use on your project. Most of the time, some options (like alignment, offsetting, nesting…) are ignored by the developer and tend to be replaced by manual padding or margin unnecessarily.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e Before working on each components of your website, you can \\u003cstrong\\u003ebuild every templates\\u003c/strong\\u003e used in the creatives only with the grid classes. Building the structure before everything else, will facilitate your work afterwards.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"highlight highlight-text-html-basic Some webfonts are limited in terms of pageviews and can’t be hosted (\\u003ca href=\\\"https://aeolidia.com/understanding-webfont-licensing-structures/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eUnderstanding to show each different pages, if multiple views are provided within the same PSD. It’s an easy way to avoid confusions and check that all elements are correctly organized.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003ca href=\\\"#table-of-contents\\\"\\u003e⬆ back to top\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e2. - Analysis and pre-work phases\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-2---analysis-and-pre-work-phases\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 2. - Analysis and pre-work phases\\\" href=\\\"#2---analysis-and-pre-work-phases\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca target=\\\"_blank\\\" rel=\\\"noopener noreferrer\\\" href=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/phases.png\\\"\\u003e\\u003cimg src=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/phases.png\\\" alt=\\\"Analysis and phases\\\" style=\\\"max-width: 100%;\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eBefore starting the analysis and the pre-work phases and after receiving the creative files, you need to check some important elements:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003cem\\u003eWhich version of Photoshop, Sketch is used?\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\nSome features are specific to some versions of Photoshop or Sketch. It is important to flag any issue regarding this as soon as possible.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003cem\\u003eIs the width of each PSD or artboard correct?\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\nIn case some space is added on each side of the design, check the exact width of the website.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003cem\\u003eAre the creatives using too much “box-shadow”, “linear or radial gradient”…?\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\nDon’t forget the .... Effect which can have impacts on the browser painting performance.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003cem\\u003eIs a sitemap / breadcrumb provided to understand the architecture of all pages and their dependencies?\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003cem\\u003eDoes the website needs to have retina images?\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003ca href=\\\"#table-of-contents\\\"\\u003e⬆ back to top\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e2.1 - Paper analysis\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-21---paper-analysis\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 2.1 - Paper analysis\\\" href=\\\"#21---paper-analysis\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca target=\\\"_blank\\\" rel=\\\"noopener noreferrer\\\" href=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/analysis.png\\\"\\u003e\\u003cimg src=\\\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/analysis.png\\\" alt=\\\"Paper Analysis\\\" style=\\\"max-width: 100%;\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eIt is recommended \\u003cstrong\\u003eprinting\\u003c/strong\\u003e some (or all) of the pages you have on an A3 format (or A4 if you don’t have this format). Because of the height of the page. you’ll probably need to print some designs on multiple pages.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eI can’t imagine a better way to start than analysing creatives on a paper with a pencil (or different colourful pencils chosen to highlight different type of information).\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003col dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eDefine the \\u003cstrong\\u003estructure of the pages\\u003c/strong\\u003e, the headers, the sections, the articles, main, footer outlining these on at least one printed page.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eFind all the \\u003cstrong\\u003eheadings\\u003c/strong\\u003e that structured a page, ensure the \\u003ccode\\u003eH1\\u003c/code\\u003e is not on the logo and that the logical order is followed. Most of the time, the H1 for the homepage will be hidden with CSS but needs to keep its legitimate meaning. That analysis should be done with the help of a SEO specialist in case you have one in your team.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eTry to find and regroup \\u003cstrong\\u003esimilar components\\u003c/strong\\u003e giving them an individual name regarding their functionality and not just their context. For example, naming a tab system “\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eMost of the creative elements can be \\u003cstrong\\u003edone using CSS\\u003c/strong\\u003e. Today, it is not recommended to create any layout element using images. Any simple graphical element like buttons or borders should be done in CSS to avoid performance or scalability issues.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eFind some \\u003cstrong\\u003epossible lack of coherence\\u003c/strong\\u003e, in case a Styleguide was not provided by the creative team, it’s your responsibility to ensure that every graphic element belong to a possible category (Buttons, Typography, Sliders…). It’ll help you to create your own CSS / Sass architecture or to identify which component you’ll need from an identified CSS Framework.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ol\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cg-emoji class=\\\"g-emoji\\\" alias=\\\"warning\\\"\\u003e⚠️\\u003c/g-emoji\\u003e \\u003cem\\u003eAfter the paper analysis phase, you can invite the creative team to use a tool like \\u003ca href=\\\"https://www.invisionapp.com/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eInVision\\u003c/a\\u003e, to facilitate the communication and exchange between the creative team and the developers. The possibility to comment directly on pages can be a time-saver and allow to keep a history of modifications and decisions.\\u003c/em\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch3 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e2.2 - Pre-development phase\\u003c/h3\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-22---pre-development-phase\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 2.2 - Pre-development phase\\\" href=\\\"#22---pre-development-phase\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cul class=\\\"contains-task-list\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e According to the specifications, \\u003cstrong\\u003eplugins needed were defined\\u003c/strong\\u003e in an early stage. Having a pre-list of possible plugins before starting the development can help the developer to stay focus and not spend too much time in doing research during the development phase. Obviously, some plugins may not perfectly fit and will be changed accordingly.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eAdditional Resources:\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e🛠 \\u003ca href=\\\"https://js.libhunt.com/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eAwesome JS\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e🛠 \\u003ca href=\\\"https://bestof.js.org/\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eBestOfJS\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003ca href=\\\"#table-of-contents\\\"\\u003e⬆ back to top\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e3. - Validation\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-3---validation\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 3. - Validation\\\" href=\\\"#3---validation\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThe validation phase is when everything seems to be ready to be integrated. The client, in general, validate the creatives without waiting for any approval from the technical team. As exposed in the Design Checklist, it is essential that developers ensure the quality of the delivery before starting to code.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e4. - Development phase\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-4---development-phase\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: 4. - Development phase\\\" href=\\\"#4---development-phase\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cul class=\\\"contains-task-list\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e All \\u003cstrong\\u003emedias can be cut and saved\\u003c/strong\\u003e before starting the development phase. That can help you to avoid back and forth between your creative software and your code editor.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e \\u003cstrong\\u003eThe image folder has a clear architecture\\u003c/strong\\u003e where you  arranged the layout's images. It is important to stay consistent between projects in general. Defining a structure for that folder and a naming convention can be helpful.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eYou can find an example of a possible structure with prefixes used to recognise each image appurtenance.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\\\".\\n└── images\\n    ├── background\\n    ├── banners\\n    ├── icons\\n    └── layout\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpre\\u003e\\u003cspan class=\\\"pl-c1\\\"\\u003e.\\u003c/span\\u003e\\n└── images\\n    ├── background\\n    ├── banners\\n    ├── icons\\n    └── layout\\u003c/pre\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cul class=\\\"contains-task-list\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli class=\\\"task-list-item\\\"\\u003e\\u003cinput type=\\\"checkbox\\\" id=\\\"\\\" disabled=\\\"\\\" class=\\\"task-list-item-checkbox\\\" aria-label=\\\"Incomplete task\\\"\\u003e \\u003cstrong\\u003eA naming convention is used\\u003c/strong\\u003e like adding prefixes to differentiate types of images, all images used for background can be prefixed by \\u003ccode\\u003ebg-\\u003c/code\\u003e, icons by \\u003ccode\\u003eicon-\\u003c/code\\u003e, hero banners by \\u003ccode\\u003ehero-\\u003c/code\\u003e or \\u003ccode\\u003ebanner-\\u003c/code\\u003e and so on.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" Validation</a></strong></li>\n<li><strong><a href=\"#4---development-phase\">4. Development phase</a></strong></li>\n<li><strong><a href=\"#5---before-production\">5. Before production</a></strong></li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip\" dir=\"auto\"><p class=\"markdown-alert-title\" dir=\"auto\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z\"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir=\"auto\">⭐️ I've just launched a new project for <a href=\"https://git.new/uxpatterns\" rel=\"nofollow\">UX developers about patterns</a>, feel free to check it out! ⭐️</p>\n</div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">In case you are looking for a list of all elements you need to have/to test before launching your site/HTML page to production, take a look on the → <a href=\"https://github.com/thedaviddias/Front-End-Checklist\">Front-End Checklist</a>.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">How to use the Design Checklist?</h2><a id=\"user-content-how-to-use-the-design-checklist\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How to use the Design Checklist?\" href=\"#how-to-use-the-design-checklist\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">When comes the moment where developers discover new web designs, before converting them to code, some important elements may be missing. The Front-End Design Checklist is a tool for Front-End developers and Web Designers which aim to help both to work in a seamlessly way.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">You can share that checklist to Web Designers to ensure time will be saved at the delivery time or you can use it to review all elements delivered by the creative team and ensure everything is correct before digging into the code integration.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Why you need to use the Design Checklist?</h2><a id=\"user-content-why-you-need-to-use-the-design-checklist\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why you need to use the Design Checklist?\" href=\"#why-you-need-to-use-the-design-checklist\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Ensure all points are taken into consideration by the Creative Team</li>\n<li>Having a document where Web Designers and Developers can rely to ensure a better communication and coherence in the way they interact.</li>\n<li>Because it's easy to forget some important elements when you are pushed by short timelines</li>\n<li>Avoid discovering issues after the creative team is already working on another project.</li>\n<li>To show the complementary work between a Web Designer and a Front-End Developer</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1. - Design requirements</h2><a id=\"user-content-1---design-requirements\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1. - Design requirements\" href=\"#1---design-requirements\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Designing a website or a webapp requires following some rules and taking into consideration that the project is not only a graphic project but a web project too. The next sections are crucial for any web project.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.1 - Grid system</h3><a id=\"user-content-11---grid-system\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.1 - Grid system\" href=\"#11---grid-system\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/grid-system.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/grid-system.png\" alt=\"Grid system\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> A <strong>grid</strong> is explicitly provided in the design, and the details of the grid are present in the technical specification (width, gutters, number of columns…). The Web Designer can keep the grid in a transparent layer and use it on all his project.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ℹ️ <a href=\"https://guideguide.me/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Guide Guide</a> is a plugin for Photoshop that can help you easily build your grid.</p>\n</blockquote>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ℹ️ On Sketch, you can use the integrated “<a href=\"https://www.sketchapp.com/docs/canvas/rulers-guides-grids/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Make Grid Tool</a>” to design your desired grid.</p>\n</blockquote>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>Be familiar with the grid system</strong> you’ll use on your project. Most of the time, some options (like alignment, offsetting, nesting…) are ignored by the developer and tend to be replaced by manual padding or margin unnecessarily.</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> Before working on each components of your website, you can <strong>build every templates</strong> used in the creatives only with the grid classes. Building the structure before everything else, will facilitate your work afterwards.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"&lt;div class=&quot;container&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;div class=&quot;row&quot;&gt;\n    &lt;div class=&quot;col-sm&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;!-- Let empty at first --&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;div class=&quot;col-sm&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;!-- Let empty at first --&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;div class=&quot;col-sm&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;!-- Let empty at first --&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n  &lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\"><pre><span class=\"pl-kos\">&lt;</span><span class=\"pl-ent\">div</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">class</span>=\"<span class=\"pl-s\">container</span>\"<span class=\"pl-kos\">&gt;</span>\n  <span class=\"pl-kos\">&lt;</span><span class=\"pl-ent\">div</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">class</span>=\"<span class=\"pl-s\">row</span>\"<span class=\"pl-kos\">&gt;</span>\n    <span class=\"pl-kos\">&lt;</span><span class=\"pl-ent\">div</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">class</span>=\"<span class=\"pl-s\">col-sm</span>\"<span class=\"pl-kos\">&gt;</span>\n      <span class=\"pl-c\">&lt;!-- Let empty at first --&gt;</span>\n    <span class=\"pl-kos\">&lt;/</span><span class=\"pl-kos\">&lt;/</span><span class=\"pl-ent\">div</span><span class=\"pl-kos\">&gt;</span></pre></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><g-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\">⚠️</g-emoji> <em>If you want to ensure that the grid and the width of the devices are respected, you may want to generate yourself a PSD template and that you will send it to the Web Designer.</em></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://getbootstrap.com/docs/4.0/layout/grid/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Bootstrap Grid System</a> (v4)</li>\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"http://flexboxgrid.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Flexbox Grid</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://css-tricks.com/dont-overthink-it-grids/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Don't Overthink It Grids | CSS-Tricks</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.2 - Colors</h3><a id=\"user-content-12---colors\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.2 - Colors\" href=\"#12---colors\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/colors.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/colors.png\" alt=\"Colors\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>All colors used in the creatives are named</strong> ($gray-light, $gray-dark, $green) or accordingly with their use ($body-background, $body-copy, $text-paragraph…). They can be exported in an <a href=\"https://www.lifewire.com/aco-file-2619477\" rel=\"nofollow\">ACO file</a> (if use Photoshop or on a symbol page for Sketch) and shared with the developers.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/color-swatches.jpg\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/color-swatches.jpg\" alt=\"Color Swatches\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The different <strong>color state</strong> of some elements (like buttons, links, inputs...) are defined and worked in the context of a light or dark background and with a light or a dark text.</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All or the most important/used <strong>colors are accessible</strong> in the design to allow a fluid navigation on the website/webapp.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://contrastchecker.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">WCAG - Contrast Checker</a></li>\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"http://colorsafe.co/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Color Safe - accessible web color combinations</a></li>\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://coolors.co/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Coolors.co - The super fast color schemes generator</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.3 - Fonts and texts</h3><a id=\"user-content-13---fonts-and-texts\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.3 - Fonts and texts\" href=\"#13---fonts-and-texts\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/fonts.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/fonts.png\" alt=\"Fonts\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Fonts are an essential part of every design, they shouldn’t be chosen without discernment. Choosing the wrong font for a project could have financial and legal impacts.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">It is recommended to ask your client to buy these fonts to avoid possible future issues and take into consideration the condition of use. Some webfonts are limited in terms of pageviews and can’t be hosted (<a href=\"https://aeolidia.com/understanding-webfont-licensing-structures/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Understanding Webfont Licensing Structures</a>).</p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The fonts for desktop (TTF or OTF in general) and the webfonts, in <strong>WOFF, WOFF2 and TTF format</strong> were provided (in a Zip file or given access to the website where they were bought).</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ℹ️ TTF format for desktop is not the same than TTF for Web.</p>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://css-tricks.com/snippets/css/using-font-face/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Using @font-face | CSS-Tricks</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fallback_font\" rel=\"nofollow\">Fallback font</a> stacks were specified in a document (ideally the Style Guide) to the Front-End Developer.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://css-tricks.com/css-basics-fallback-font-stacks-robust-web-typography/\" rel=\"nofollow\">CSS Basics: Fallback Font Stacks for More Robust Web Typography | CSS-Tricks</a></li>\n<li><a href=\"https://www.fontsquirrel.com/tools/webfont-generator\" rel=\"nofollow\">Create Your Own @font-face Kits » Font Squirrel</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>total weight</strong> of the all webfonts don’t exceed 1-2 Mb (all variants included: italic, bold etc).</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> As far as possible, <strong>all texts are provided in the proper language</strong> instead of dummy texts in English (Lorem Ipsum and affiliates).</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ℹ️ In case of a multilingual website, always ask yourself how the design can react if the text is longer than it was previously define. Remember that Web Designers use to create perfect designs and don’t always think about possible issues or situation with too much text.</p>\n</blockquote>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://developers.google.com/web/fundamentals/performance/optimizing-content-efficiency/webfont-optimization\" rel=\"nofollow\">Web Font Optimization &nbsp;|&nbsp; Web Fundamentals &nbsp;|&nbsp; Google Developers</a></li>\n<li><a href=\"https://css-tricks.com/font-display-masses/\" rel=\"nofollow\"><code>font-display</code> for the Masses | CSS-Tricks</a></li>\n<li><a href=\"https://betterwebtype.com/rhythm-in-web-typography\" rel=\"nofollow\">Rhythm in Typography | improve legibility, readability, and visual hierarchy</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.4 - Links and navigation</h3><a id=\"user-content-14---links-and-navigation\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.4 - Links and navigation\" href=\"#14---links-and-navigation\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/links.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/links.png\" alt=\"Links and navigation\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All <strong>links have a default, hover, focus, active and visited state</strong> clearly defined (the Style Guide is the best document to specified these).</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> Alternate views of all navigation states (hover, active/current page).</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.5 - Images / Icons</h3><a id=\"user-content-15---images--icons\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.5 - Images / Icons\" href=\"#15---images--icons\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/images.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/images.png\" alt=\"Images\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> A <strong>favicon image</strong> with at least 512px X 512px is provided in PNG format. The generation of all the others Favicons can be easily done with online tools.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><a href=\"https://realfavicongenerator.net/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Favicon Generator for all platforms: iOS, Android, PC/Mac...</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All icons are provided in <strong>SVG format</strong>, each in the same square dimension, in black and in a separated folder.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://jakearchibald.github.io/svgomg/\" rel=\"nofollow\">SVGOMG - SVGO's Missing GUI</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>name of each icon</strong> starts with <code>icon-</code> and is entirely in lowercase (without any space and using dashes to separate each word).</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://images.guide/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Essential Image Optimization</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.6 Forms and buttons</h3><a id=\"user-content-16-forms-and-buttons\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.6 Forms and buttons\" href=\"#16-forms-and-buttons\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/forms.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/forms.png\" alt=\"Forms\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All forms possess a title that can be used as a legend</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> An example of the <strong>different states of all input fields</strong> were provided (at least focus and inactive/disabled state).</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>All error messages</strong> were provided, the text (eventually in a separated document) the position and the color are clearly identifiable in the creatives and consistent. Some messages should be different according to the error.\n<strong>Resources:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxdesign.cc/forms-need-validation-2ecbccbacea1\" rel=\"nofollow\">Forms Need Validation – UX Collective</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>Indicators of required/optional</strong> fields are provided.</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>primary and secondary buttons</strong> are clearly identifiable and are used following common practices.\n<strong>Resources:</strong>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxplanet.org/primary-secondary-action-buttons-c16df9b36150\" rel=\"nofollow\">Primary &amp; Secondary Action Buttons – UX Planet</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> An example of the <strong>different states of a button</strong> were provided (Normal, hover, focused, pressed and inactive state).</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> Buttons with built-in <strong>loading indicators</strong> are provided and can be applied to any button.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxdesign.cc/design-better-forms-96fadca0f49c\" rel=\"nofollow\">Design Better Forms – UX Collective</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxdesign.cc/design-better-input-fields-3d02985a8e24\" rel=\"nofollow\">Design Better Input Fields – UX Collective</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxplanet.org/designing-perfect-text-field-clarity-accessibility-and-user-effort-d03c1e26004b\" rel=\"nofollow\">Designing Perfect Text Field: Clarity, Accessibility and User Effort</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://uxplanet.org/button-ux-design-best-practices-types-and-states-647cf4ae0fc6\" rel=\"nofollow\">Button UX Design: Best Practices, Types and States – UX Planet</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://www.smashingmagazine.com/2016/11/a-quick-guide-for-designing-better-buttons/\" rel=\"nofollow\">How To Design Better Buttons — Smashing Magazine</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://medium.com/eightshapes-llc/buttons-in-design-systems-eac3acf7e23\" rel=\"nofollow\">Buttons in Design Systems – EightShapes – Medium</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.7 - Responsive Web Design</h3><a id=\"user-content-17---responsive-web-design\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.7 - Responsive Web Design\" href=\"#17---responsive-web-design\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/responsive.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/responsive.png\" alt=\"Responsive\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>mobile version</strong> of the design is provided before or at the same time of the desktop version.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">If the “<strong>mobile first</strong>” thinking was not followed by the creative team, some irregularities and inconsistencies may appear between the mobile and the desktop version. Check and flag these issues before starting the development of the project.</p>\n</blockquote>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>tablet version</strong> of the design in certain cases should be provide too.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><g-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\">⚠️</g-emoji> <em>The <strong>pixel perfect</strong> notion is today in a certain way deprecated. Today, it’s impossible to have a design that worked the same facing the multitude of the screen sizes.</em></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://webmasters.googleblog.com/2016/11/mobile-first-indexing.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">Official Google Webmaster Central Blog: Mobile-first Indexing</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.8 - Style Guide and component approach</h3><a id=\"user-content-18---style-guide-and-component-approach\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.8 - Style Guide and component approach\" href=\"#18---style-guide-and-component-approach\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/styleguide.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/styleguide.png\" alt=\"Styleguide\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All components designed on each page were created with the <strong>component based approach</strong>  (Atomic Design). If not, issues can occur in terms of performance, maintainability of the project...</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"http://bradfrost.com/blog/post/atomic-web-design/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Atomic design</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://www.tandemseven.com/technology/6-reasons-component-based-ui-development/\" rel=\"nofollow\">6 Reasons for Component-Based UI Development</a></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> A <strong>Style Guide</strong> needs to be provided listing all elements, components, styles, dimensions. Some boilerplates like <a href=\"https://www.uxpower.tools/\" rel=\"nofollow\">UX Power Tools</a> can help saving time and keep consistency in the designs.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><g-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\">⚠️</g-emoji> <em>In the case where the Style Guide is missing, it's a good practice to build yourself a <a href=\"https://github.com/davidhund/styleguide-generators\">living Style Guide</a> to facilitate your work. Some CMS like Drupal, for example, have plugins that allow to develop a living Style Guide using <a href=\"https://drupal-pattern-lab.github.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Pattern Lab</a>.</em></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://medium.com/sketch-app-sources/tagged/style-guides\" rel=\"nofollow\">Style Guides – Design + Sketch – Medium</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://codepen.io/guide\" rel=\"nofollow\">The CodePen Design Patterns and Style Guide</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"http://rizzo.lonelyplanet.com/styleguide/design-elements/colours\" rel=\"nofollow\">Lonely Planet Travel Guides and Travel Information</a></li>\n<li>📖 <a href=\"https://www.yelp.com/styleguide\" rel=\"nofollow\">Styleguide</a></li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">In the case of an existing project:</h4><a id=\"user-content-in-the-case-of-an-existing-project\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: In the case of an existing project:\" href=\"#in-the-case-of-an-existing-project\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Sometimes, the creative team needs to add new pages or modules in an existing project. They should have or create a list of all existing elements and try to use what is already there. Having a Style Guide already created can save hours and ensure consistency of the project.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1.9 - Delivery files</h3><a id=\"user-content-19---delivery-files\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1.9 - Delivery files\" href=\"#19---delivery-files\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/delivery-files.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/delivery-files.png\" alt=\"Delivery files\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> For all websites, the web designer needs to provide at least <strong>2 PSD</strong> (mobile, desktop and eventually tablet) or at least <strong>1 Sketch file</strong> which needs to be delivered with the dimension below (if you have Photoshop CC 2015 and above, I recommend using artboards).</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ℹ️ Some web designers could eventually create multiple PSD corresponding to each components used and import them in a single PSD as “smart layer”. In that case, you’ll have multiple PSD linked to one or two files. In the case of Sketch, since the <strong>libraries</strong> exist since version 47, it is possible to link multiples files with symbols ……..</p>\n</blockquote>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>creative files are cleaned</strong> before delivering to developers (empty and unnecessary layer needs to be removed to avoid large files).</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> The <strong>404 error</strong> (and eventually the page 500 error) page were designed.</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All <strong>popins, popups and alert boxes</strong> were designed and can be enable throw layers of compositions.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>📖 <a href=\"http://photoshopetiquette.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Photoshop Etiquette: A Guide to Discernible Web Design</a></li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Specific rules for PSD file:</h4><a id=\"user-content-specific-rules-for-psd-file\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Specific rules for PSD file:\" href=\"#specific-rules-for-psd-file\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>Layer compositions</strong> are used to show each different pages, if multiple views are provided within the same PSD. It’s an easy way to avoid confusions and check that all elements are correctly organized.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">2. - Analysis and pre-work phases</h2><a id=\"user-content-2---analysis-and-pre-work-phases\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. - Analysis and pre-work phases\" href=\"#2---analysis-and-pre-work-phases\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/phases.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/phases.png\" alt=\"Analysis and phases\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Before starting the analysis and the pre-work phases and after receiving the creative files, you need to check some important elements:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong><em>Which version of Photoshop, Sketch is used?</em></strong>\nSome features are specific to some versions of Photoshop or Sketch. It is important to flag any issue regarding this as soon as possible.</li>\n<li><strong><em>Is the width of each PSD or artboard correct?</em></strong>\nIn case some space is added on each side of the design, check the exact width of the website.</li>\n<li><strong><em>Are the creatives using too much “box-shadow”, “linear or radial gradient”…?</em></strong>\nDon’t forget the .... Effect which can have impacts on the browser painting performance.</li>\n<li><strong><em>Is a sitemap / breadcrumb provided to understand the architecture of all pages and their dependencies?</em></strong></li>\n<li><strong><em>Does the website needs to have retina images?</em></strong></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">2.1 - Paper analysis</h3><a id=\"user-content-21---paper-analysis\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2.1 - Paper analysis\" href=\"#21---paper-analysis\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/blob/master/images/analysis.png\"><img src=\"/thedaviddias/Front-End-Design-Checklist/raw/master/images/analysis.png\" alt=\"Paper Analysis\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">It is recommended <strong>printing</strong> some (or all) of the pages you have on an A3 format (or A4 if you don’t have this format). Because of the height of the page. you’ll probably need to print some designs on multiple pages.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">I can’t imagine a better way to start than analysing creatives on a paper with a pencil (or different colourful pencils chosen to highlight different type of information).</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Define the <strong>structure of the pages</strong>, the headers, the sections, the articles, main, footer outlining these on at least one printed page.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Find all the <strong>headings</strong> that structured a page, ensure the <code>H1</code> is not on the logo and that the logical order is followed. Most of the time, the H1 for the homepage will be hidden with CSS but needs to keep its legitimate meaning. That analysis should be done with the help of a SEO specialist in case you have one in your team.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Try to find and regroup <strong>similar components</strong> giving them an individual name regarding their functionality and not just their context. For example, naming a tab system “</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Most of the creative elements can be <strong>done using CSS</strong>. Today, it is not recommended to create any layout element using images. Any simple graphical element like buttons or borders should be done in CSS to avoid performance or scalability issues.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Find some <strong>possible lack of coherence</strong>, in case a Styleguide was not provided by the creative team, it’s your responsibility to ensure that every graphic element belong to a possible category (Buttons, Typography, Sliders…). It’ll help you to create your own CSS / Sass architecture or to identify which component you’ll need from an identified CSS Framework.</p>\n</li>\n</ol>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><g-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\">⚠️</g-emoji> <em>After the paper analysis phase, you can invite the creative team to use a tool like <a href=\"https://www.invisionapp.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">InVision</a>, to facilitate the communication and exchange between the creative team and the developers. The possibility to comment directly on pages can be a time-saver and allow to keep a history of modifications and decisions.</em></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">2.2 - Pre-development phase</h3><a id=\"user-content-22---pre-development-phase\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2.2 - Pre-development phase\" href=\"#22---pre-development-phase\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> According to the specifications, <strong>plugins needed were defined</strong> in an early stage. Having a pre-list of possible plugins before starting the development can help the developer to stay focus and not spend too much time in doing research during the development phase. Obviously, some plugins may not perfectly fit and will be changed accordingly.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Additional Resources:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://js.libhunt.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Awesome JS</a></li>\n<li>🛠 <a href=\"https://bestof.js.org/\" rel=\"nofollow\">BestOfJS</a></li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">3. - Validation</h2><a id=\"user-content-3---validation\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. - Validation\" href=\"#3---validation\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The validation phase is when everything seems to be ready to be integrated. The client, in general, validate the creatives without waiting for any approval from the technical team. As exposed in the Design Checklist, it is essential that developers ensure the quality of the delivery before starting to code.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">4. - Development phase</h2><a id=\"user-content-4---development-phase\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4. - Development phase\" href=\"#4---development-phase\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> All <strong>medias can be cut and saved</strong> before starting the development phase. That can help you to avoid back and forth between your creative software and your code editor.</p>\n</li>\n<li class=\"task-list-item\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>The image folder has a clear architecture</strong> where you  arranged the layout's images. It is important to stay consistent between projects in general. Defining a structure for that folder and a naming convention can be helpful.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">You can find an example of a possible structure with prefixes used to recognise each image appurtenance.</p>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\".\n└── images\n    ├── background\n    ├── banners\n    ├── icons\n    └── layout\"><pre><span class=\"pl-c1\">.</span>\n└── images\n    ├── background\n    ├── banners\n    ├── icons\n    └── layout</pre></div>\n<ul class=\"contains-task-list\">\n<li class=\"task-list-item\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"> <strong>A naming convention is used</strong> like adding prefixes to differentiate types of images, all images used for background can be prefixed by <code>bg-</code>, icons by <code>icon-</code>, hero banners by <code>hero-</code> or <code>banner-</code> and so on.</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">5. - Before production</h2><a id=\"user-content-5---before-production\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 5. - Before production\" href=\"#5---before-production\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Before launching your website, be sure to review all your pages using the <a href=\"https://frontendchecklist.io\" rel=\"nofollow\">Front-End Checklist</a>!</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong><a href=\"#table-of-contents\">⬆ back to top</a></strong></p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2
